local E=table.concat(C)
local O={}
local B,N=0,0
for i=1,#E do
    local c=E:sub(i,i)
    if c~="=" then
        B=B*64+M[c]
        N=N+6
        if N>=8 then
            N=N-8
            local v=math.floor(B/(2^N))%256
            B=B%(2^N)
            local j=#O+1
            O[j]=string.char((v-((j*73+41)%256))%256)
        end
    end
end
local compiler=loadstring
if type(compiler)~="function" then error("This obfuscated build requires an executor with loadstring support",0) end
local fn,err=compiler(table.concat(O))
if not fn then error(err,0) end
if type(getfenv)=="function" and type(setfenv)=="function" then
    pcall(setfenv,fn,getfenv())
end
return fn()
